# phase-11 完整对话

分配 413 词形；已接受 413，暂缓 0，未决 0。

“已接受”仅指本版本存在完整、绑定文本哈希的独立署名审阅记录，不是程序证明语义正确。

<a id="dialogue-p11-a-01"></a>

## 商定软件发布前的补充演练 · p11-a-01

主题：工作与协作；情景：商定软件发布前的补充演练（release-rehearsal-checkpoint）。

沟通目标：明确上次演练漏查的环节，并约定在测试环境演练新的检查步骤

背景：软件团队在发布前复核演练清单；本段只讨论测试安排，不执行真实发布。

人物：Mara、Dev。

起草：author-phase11-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p11-a-01-s01 · Mara: With the release imminent, can we fit in one last rehearsal?
- p11-a-01-s02 · Dev: Yes, but let's execute the checklist in the test environment, not on the live service.
- p11-a-01-s03 · Mara: The last rehearsal showed a gap in our execution: nobody checked the restored files.
- p11-a-01-s04 · Dev: Then implementation of the new backup step needs an owner.
- p11-a-01-s05 · Mara: I'll check the restored files. Can you watch the error log while I do that?
- p11-a-01-s06 · Dev: Of course. We'll rehearse those steps together before we decide whether we're ready.

目标用法：

- imminent · adj. · 即将发生的；义项 sense-staged-ox-b1469952870620f8-p11-a-01；来源 ox-b1469952870620f8；证据 p11-a-01-s01；词形 imminent。
- execute · v. · 执行；义项 sense-staged-ox-9a21945b7538693b-p11-a-01；来源 ox-9a21945b7538693b；证据 p11-a-01-s02；词形 execute。
- execution · n. · 执行过程；义项 sense-staged-ox-2b6e6c8ac328e4c6-p11-a-01；来源 ox-2b6e6c8ac328e4c6；证据 p11-a-01-s03；词形 execution。
- implementation · n. · 实施；义项 sense-staged-ox-46ef57772916e90b-p11-a-01；来源 ox-46ef57772916e90b；证据 p11-a-01-s04；词形 implementation。

审阅者：reviewer-phase11-a；整篇结论：accepted。

完整对话从恢复文件漏查转向测试环境补演；Mara 与 Dev 的分工在 s05–s06 明确接受。标题、情景标签与两项结果证据均只声称约定演练，不称已发布。

绑定文件 SHA-256：23caab9dc524ee84f13d6e6b1528882fed2db954e997893b040913e98aaab5dc

- ox-b1469952870620f8：accepted；s01 用形容词 imminent 说明软件发布迫近，给最后一次演练提供动机。（p11-a-01-s01）。
- ox-9a21945b7538693b：accepted；s02 的 execute the checklist 是按清单执行步骤的动词搭配，并明确限定测试环境。（p11-a-01-s02）。
- ox-2b6e6c8ac328e4c6：accepted；s03 的 a gap in our execution 指清单实施过程中的漏检，不是死刑或代码执行的混淆。（p11-a-01-s03）。
- ox-46ef57772916e90b：accepted；s04 名词 implementation of the new backup step 指落实新增步骤，需要明确负责人。（p11-a-01-s04）。

<a id="dialogue-p11-a-02"></a>

## 约定暂停科学展台并请负责人检查 · p11-a-02

主题：学习与技能；情景：约定暂停科学展台并请负责人检查（science-display-safety-pause）。

沟通目标：指出模型异常及展台安全边界，并约定暂停演示、请负责人检查

背景：虚构科学馆的工作人员检查一个氢能模型展台；展台不储存氢气，安全要求来自本场景给定的展台说明。

人物：Inez、Colin。

起草：author-phase11-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p11-a-02-s01 · Inez: Is this the experimental display about hydrogen?
- p11-a-02-s02 · Colin: Yes. It uses a model, not real gas. The sign explains that hydrogen can form an explosive mixture with air.
- p11-a-02-s03 · Inez: The model's cover has come loose. Could that be a hazard for visitors?
- p11-a-02-s04 · Colin: It could. The display instructions say to halt the demonstration if any cover is loose.
- p11-a-02-s05 · Inez: Then let's leave it off and ask the supervisor to inspect it before anyone uses it.
- p11-a-02-s06 · Colin: Agreed. I'll contact her now and keep visitors away from this table.

目标用法：

- experimental · adj. · 试验性的；义项 sense-staged-ox-f813b9b872b1eb61-p11-a-02；来源 ox-f813b9b872b1eb61；证据 p11-a-02-s01；词形 experimental。
- hydrogen · n. · 氢；义项 sense-staged-ox-287451bce21782bc-p11-a-02；来源 ox-287451bce21782bc；证据 p11-a-02-s01；词形 hydrogen。
- explosive · adj. · 易爆炸的；义项 sense-staged-ox-48c61b97fe963400-p11-a-02；来源 ox-48c61b97fe963400；证据 p11-a-02-s02；词形 explosive。
- hazard · n. · 危险隐患；义项 sense-staged-ox-773b77440b049e7b-p11-a-02；来源 ox-773b77440b049e7b；证据 p11-a-02-s03；词形 hazard。
- halt · v. · 停止；义项 sense-staged-ox-61a630c3e0943bd0-p11-a-02；来源 ox-61a630c3e0943bd0；证据 p11-a-02-s04；词形 halt。

审阅者：reviewer-phase11-a；整篇结论：accepted。

氢能模型不含真实气体，松动盖板而非氢气是当前风险；双方按场景给定说明约定停演并请主管检查。标题、标签和主张没有把联系或检查写成已经完成。

绑定文件 SHA-256：117517e4387a1f994f63ea5099e2bef491bc1489eff896df241288e49d8855d8

- ox-f813b9b872b1eb61：accepted；s01 experimental 修饰该科学展台，采用试验性模型展示的形容词用法。（p11-a-02-s01）。
- ox-287451bce21782bc：accepted；s01 名词 hydrogen 是展台讨论的物质主题；s02 随即排除现场真实气体。（p11-a-02-s01）。
- ox-48c61b97fe963400：accepted；s02 explosive mixture 形容可爆炸混合物，作为标牌背景说明而非操作指令。（p11-a-02-s02）。
- ox-773b77440b049e7b：accepted；s03 a hazard for visitors 指松盖给参观者带来的危险隐患，名词与语境一致。（p11-a-02-s03）。
- ox-61a630c3e0943bd0：accepted；s04 halt the demonstration 是说明中停止演示的动词要求。（p11-a-02-s04）。

<a id="dialogue-p11-a-03"></a>

## 明确志愿评委独立打分的边界 · p11-a-03

主题：社区与公共空间；情景：明确志愿评委独立打分的边界（volunteer-scoring-pressure-boundary）。

沟通目标：说明评分公平要求，并约定在评委说明中写明不得施压

背景：社区故事比赛的组织者审阅志愿评委说明。

人物：Tessa、Malik。

起草：author-phase11-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p11-a-03-s01 · Tessa: A sponsor wants us to tell the judges which entrant she supports.
- p11-a-03-s02 · Malik: That would undermine fairness. We can't exert pressure on volunteers to favor a particular entrant.
- p11-a-03-s03 · Tessa: The current guide just says to be respectful. We need something more explicit.
- p11-a-03-s04 · Malik: How about: judges must score independently, and sponsors must not contact them about scores?
- p11-a-03-s05 · Tessa: Yes. Please state explicitly that this applies to the organizing team too.
- p11-a-03-s06 · Malik: I will. I'll add both points to the next version and send it to you for a check.

目标用法：

- fairness · n. · 公正；义项 sense-staged-ox-25f72f238a3a6dd8-p11-a-03；来源 ox-25f72f238a3a6dd8；证据 p11-a-03-s02；词形 fairness。
- exert · v. · 施加；义项 sense-staged-ox-edf0dc66639d3c52-p11-a-03；来源 ox-edf0dc66639d3c52；证据 p11-a-03-s02；词形 exert。
- explicit · adj. · 明确的；义项 sense-staged-ox-6754b930179302af-p11-a-03；来源 ox-6754b930179302af；证据 p11-a-03-s03；词形 explicit。
- explicitly · adv. · 明确地；义项 sense-staged-ox-d15ffba90a81aa42-p11-a-03；来源 ox-d15ffba90a81aa42；证据 p11-a-03-s05；词形 explicitly。

审阅者：reviewer-phase11-a；整篇结论：accepted。

赞助者干预引出独立评分边界，组织者同样受约束；s05–s06 约定写入下一版供检查。明确边界已完成与文稿尚待修改在私有主张中区分准确。

绑定文件 SHA-256：fbae8feba630693e80dce15478a18d63cb9187c748410809e195c8cd79f76bfd

- ox-25f72f238a3a6dd8：accepted；s02 undermine fairness 将评分公正作为不能受干预的原则，抽象名词自然。（p11-a-03-s02）。
- ox-edf0dc66639d3c52：accepted；s02 exert pressure on volunteers 是对志愿评委施压的标准动词搭配。（p11-a-03-s02）。
- ox-6754b930179302af：accepted；s03 something more explicit 要求比笼统尊重更明确的规则，形容词用法成立。（p11-a-03-s03）。
- ox-d15ffba90a81aa42：accepted；s05 state explicitly 修饰写明规则的方式，副词与义项吻合。（p11-a-03-s05）。

<a id="dialogue-p11-a-04"></a>

## 核对小说人物的流亡经历与未知结局 · p11-a-04

主题：文娱与运动；情景：核对小说人物的流亡经历与未知结局（fictional-exile-plot-summary）。

沟通目标：区分小说已交代的离乡经历和未交代的结局，并商定简介的表述

背景：读书会成员依据虚构小说《河的另一端》前四章修订无剧透简介。

人物：Lena、Owen。

起草：author-phase11-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p11-a-04-s01 · Lena: My summary says that Sora returns to her homeland at the end of chapter four.
- p11-a-04-s02 · Owen: She only hopes to. She's still living in exile when that chapter ends.
- p11-a-04-s03 · Lena: Right. She had to flee after the border guards came looking for her brother.
- p11-a-04-s04 · Owen: And we don't know her brother's fate. The last letter just says he left the village.
- p11-a-04-s05 · Lena: Then I'll write: Sora longs to return home while waiting for news of her brother.
- p11-a-04-s06 · Owen: That fits the chapters we have read. Keep the return out of the summary.

目标用法：

- homeland · n. · 祖国；故乡；义项 sense-staged-ox-6fcc061fc024a435-p11-a-04；来源 ox-6fcc061fc024a435；证据 p11-a-04-s01；词形 homeland。
- exile · n. · 流亡；义项 sense-staged-ox-8e5625d238b724e7-p11-a-04；来源 ox-8e5625d238b724e7；证据 p11-a-04-s02；词形 exile。
- flee · v. · 逃离；义项 sense-staged-ox-1feae6573ede0721-p11-a-04；来源 ox-1feae6573ede0721；证据 p11-a-04-s03；词形 flee。
- fate · n. · 命运；最终遭遇；义项 sense-staged-ox-09a6675455cb466f-p11-a-04；来源 ox-09a6675455cb466f；证据 p11-a-04-s04；词形 fate。

审阅者：reviewer-phase11-a；整篇结论：accepted。

读者纠正简介把希望返乡误写为已返乡的错误，并保留兄弟结局未知。s05–s06 接受新的简介方向；所有标题及目标主张支持充分，未把将修改当成已改。

绑定文件 SHA-256：82ab4551f028513aced5a0ef04cc10755cfdcd63fe788cacff35622136ba7d84

- ox-6fcc061fc024a435：accepted；s01 return to her homeland 中名词指小说人物的故乡；被纠正的是返乡事实，不是词义。（p11-a-04-s01）。
- ox-8e5625d238b724e7：accepted；s02 living in exile 明确指离乡流亡状态，名词自然。（p11-a-04-s02）。
- ox-1feae6573ede0721：accepted；s03 had to flee 指因边防搜寻而逃离，动词原因由上下文交代。（p11-a-04-s03）。
- ox-09a6675455cb466f：accepted；s04 her brother’s fate 是尚不明确的最终遭遇，与最后信件的有限消息相对。（p11-a-04-s04）。

<a id="dialogue-p11-a-05"></a>

## 商定虚构博物馆预算中的采购延期 · p11-a-05

主题：工作与协作；情景：商定虚构博物馆预算中的采购延期（fictional-museum-budget-gap）。

沟通目标：区分总收入与可用余额，核对预算缺口并决定推迟展柜采购

背景：财务沟通练习：两名学员扮演虚构博物馆的项目人员，使用给定的预算数字，不提供现实财务建议。

人物：Priya、Ben。

起草：author-phase11-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p11-a-05-s01 · Priya: The gross income for this fiscal year is eighty thousand. Why do we have only twelve thousand left?
- p11-a-05-s02 · Ben: Because gross income is before costs. Our expenditure so far is sixty-eight thousand.
- p11-a-05-s03 · Priya: The new display cases would cost fifteen thousand, so we are three thousand short.
- p11-a-05-s04 · Ben: And ordering them now would incur a storage charge, because the gallery is still closed.
- p11-a-05-s05 · Priya: Let's postpone that purchase and keep using the existing cases.
- p11-a-05-s06 · Ben: Agreed. I will revise the draft budget on that basis.

目标用法：

- gross · adj. · 未扣除费用的；总的；义项 sense-staged-ox-d3dffaaafdec2edd-p11-a-05；来源 ox-d3dffaaafdec2edd；证据 p11-a-05-s01；词形 gross。
- fiscal · adj. · 财政年度的；义项 sense-staged-ox-c1243be6adb2f616-p11-a-05；来源 ox-c1243be6adb2f616；证据 p11-a-05-s01；词形 fiscal。
- expenditure · n. · 支出；义项 sense-staged-ox-1053aa278c835744-p11-a-05；来源 ox-1053aa278c835744；证据 p11-a-05-s02；词形 expenditure。
- incur · v. · 招致；产生费用；义项 sense-staged-ox-02c47e9f9d9bf37a-p11-a-05；来源 ox-02c47e9f9d9bf37a；证据 p11-a-05-s04；词形 incur。

审阅者：reviewer-phase11-a；整篇结论：accepted。

虚构博物馆预算中八万减六万八余一万二，十五千采购缺三千；另有仓储费用风险。双方已决定延期但仅承诺改预算，标题、目标和结果状态一致。

绑定文件 SHA-256：0966d071f86b9dbf0f3e1f2fcb83ec15aec12f1f449870dcda754c745e94b3eb

- ox-d3dffaaafdec2edd：accepted；s01 gross income 表示未扣除成本的总收入，s02 明确解释了该形容词义。（p11-a-05-s01）。
- ox-c1243be6adb2f616：accepted；s01 fiscal year 指财务计量年度，形容词修饰 year 自然。（p11-a-05-s01）。
- ox-1053aa278c835744：accepted；s02 expenditure so far 是目前累计支出的名词用法，数额与后文一致。（p11-a-05-s02）。
- ox-02c47e9f9d9bf37a：accepted；s04 incur a storage charge 指采购将产生额外仓储费，动词搭配自然且保留条件性。（p11-a-05-s04）。

<a id="dialogue-p11-a-06"></a>

## 核对档案室通行证续期要求 · p11-a-06

主题：消费与服务；情景：核对档案室通行证续期要求（archive-pass-renewal-choice）。

沟通目标：确认通行证用途、到期日及证件要求，并约定携证回来办理

背景：访客在地方档案室服务台询问通行证；本场景的证件要求由工作人员明确给出。

人物：Ruth、Amir。

起草：author-phase11-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p11-a-06-s01 · Ruth: Does this pass also let me into the museum next door?
- p11-a-06-s02 · Amir: No, it is exclusively for the archive reading room.
- p11-a-06-s03 · Ruth: I see that it will expire on Friday. Can I renew it today?
- p11-a-06-s04 · Amir: Yes, if you have photo identification. We need to check the name against your record.
- p11-a-06-s05 · Ruth: Mine is at home. I'll bring it tomorrow, then.
- p11-a-06-s06 · Amir: That works. We'll check it at this desk and deal with the renewal then.

目标用法：

- exclusively · adv. · 专门地；仅限于；义项 sense-staged-ox-70459e742fa24bdb-p11-a-06；来源 ox-70459e742fa24bdb；证据 p11-a-06-s02；词形 exclusively。
- expire · v. · 到期；义项 sense-staged-ox-dfd567ae6ec19c21-p11-a-06；来源 ox-dfd567ae6ec19c21；证据 p11-a-06-s03；词形 expire。
- identification · n. · 身份证明；义项 sense-staged-ox-615dd0a6f6095152-p11-a-06；来源 ox-615dd0a6f6095152；证据 p11-a-06-s04；词形 identification。

审阅者：reviewer-phase11-a；整篇结论：accepted。

通行证仅供档案阅览室，周五到期，需要照片身份证明；工作人员接受访客明天带证回来。所述完成的是要求核对，续期办理仍是未来安排，证据没有过度承诺。

绑定文件 SHA-256：58d1e4573b0def2d7d857bc5c30953f07e2294a9e8d291645e7353f958313439

- ox-70459e742fa24bdb：accepted；s02 exclusively for the archive reading room 限定用途，副词排除隔壁博物馆。（p11-a-06-s02）。
- ox-dfd567ae6ec19c21：accepted；s03 will expire on Friday 是通行证将到期的动词用法。（p11-a-06-s03）。
- ox-615dd0a6f6095152：accepted；s04 photo identification 是需核对姓名的身份证明材料，非识别过程的混用。（p11-a-06-s04）。

<a id="dialogue-p11-a-07"></a>

## 商定先私下了解工作投诉流程 · p11-a-07

主题：工作与协作；情景：商定先私下了解工作投诉流程（fictional-worker-concern-route）。

沟通目标：说明虚构员工遭遇，并约定先获取保密投诉流程、暂不对外转述

背景：虚构职场沟通练习：同事谈及工作待遇及不受欢迎的个人评论，不作法律定性。

人物：Noor、Evan。

起草：author-phase11-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p11-a-07-s01 · Noor: They keep asking me to work unpaid after my shift. It feels like exploitation.
- p11-a-07-s02 · Evan: Have you spoken to the staff adviser?
- p11-a-07-s03 · Noor: Not yet. My supervisor also keeps making comments about my body, even after I asked him to stop.
- p11-a-07-s04 · Evan: Those comments are inappropriate. The adviser handles concerns about harassment as well as pay.
- p11-a-07-s05 · Noor: I'd like to understand the confidential reporting process before I make a formal complaint.
- p11-a-07-s06 · Evan: I can send you the adviser's contact details. I won't repeat what you've told me.
- p11-a-07-s07 · Noor: Thank you. Please send the details, and I will contact her myself.

目标用法：

- exploitation · n. · 剥削；义项 sense-staged-ox-fcc00969be0b06e9-p11-a-07；来源 ox-fcc00969be0b06e9；证据 p11-a-07-s01；词形 exploitation。
- inappropriate · adj. · 不合适的；义项 sense-staged-ox-7e2784e892aa2342-p11-a-07；来源 ox-7e2784e892aa2342；证据 p11-a-07-s04；词形 inappropriate。
- harassment · n. · 骚扰；义项 sense-staged-ox-26c80af712a3643b-p11-a-07；来源 ox-26c80af712a3643b；证据 p11-a-07-s04；词形 harassment。

审阅者：reviewer-phase11-a；整篇结论：accepted。

## 约定修正小说中派别与个人立场的概括 · p11-a-08

主题：文娱与运动；情景：约定修正小说中派别与个人立场的概括（novel-faction-motive-evidence）。

人物：Celia、Jae。

- p11-a-08-s01 · Celia: My notes describe everyone in the River Circle as an extremist.
- p11-a-08-s02 · Jae: That is too broad. One faction argues for violence, but the rest reject it.
- p11-a-08-s03 · Celia: Don't they share the same ideology about local self-rule?
- p11-a-08-s04 · Jae: They do, but that ideological agreement does not mean they agree on methods.
- p11-a-08-s05 · Celia: Then I'll identify Varo as the character who supports attacks, rather than labeling the whole group.
- p11-a-08-s06 · Jae: Good. Add that the other members challenge him in the meeting scene.

## 区分虚构案件报道中的指控与定罪 · p11-a-10

主题：学习与技能；情景：区分虚构案件报道中的指控与定罪（fictional-court-report-charge-boundary）。

沟通目标：核对案例材料中的伤亡、武器及起诉信息，并商定避免预断有罪的报道表述

背景：虚构法律报道课堂：学员审阅一份教师编造的案件摘要，所有法律类别均只属于本练习设定。

人物：Darius、Mae。

起草：author-phase11-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p11-a-10-s01 · Darius: Our case sheet describes a fatal shooting with a stolen firearm. Can my headline call the accused a killer?
- p11-a-10-s02 · Mae: No. It says the prosecutor has obtained an indictment, not a conviction.
- p11-a-10-s03 · Darius: It also lists a felony charge. I thought that settled the question of guilt.
- p11-a-10-s04 · Mae: In this exercise, a charge is an accusation. The trial has not taken place.
- p11-a-10-s05 · Darius: Then I'll say the person has been charged in connection with the shooting and leave the verdict open.
- p11-a-10-s06 · Mae: Yes. Keep the death in the report, but make the status of the case clear.

- felony · n. · 重罪；义项 sense-staged-ox-9e74cfc192a593d7-p11-a-10；来源 ox-9e74cfc192a593d7；证据 p11-a-10-s03；词形 felony。
- guilt · n. · 有罪；义项 sense-staged-ox-b69dd0a2791c29f1-p11-a-10；来源 ox-b69dd0a2791c29f1；证据 p11-a-10-s03；词形 guilt。

## 拟定读书小组轮值主持提案 · p11-a-14

主题：社区与公共空间；情景：拟定读书小组轮值主持提案（fictional-reading-group-governance）。

沟通目标：讨论主持层级对参与的影响，并约定向全组提出轮值主持试行方案

背景：虚构的女性主义读书小组成员讨论小组内部组织方式，不代表现实政治组织或政策建议。

人物：Mira、Jo。

起草：author-phase11-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p11-a-14-s01 · Mira: Our feminist reading group talks about inclusion, but the same two people lead every meeting.
- p11-a-14-s02 · Jo: That creates a hierarchy, even if nobody intended it. New members rarely get to choose a book.
- p11-a-14-s03 · Mira: Could we change the governance of the group so that the host rotates?
- p11-a-14-s04 · Jo: We should ask everyone first. Some people may prefer not to host.
- p11-a-14-s05 · Mira: Then let's propose a voluntary rotation for three meetings and review it afterward.
- p11-a-14-s06 · Jo: Agreed. I'll put that proposal on the agenda, not announce it as an adopted rule.

目标用法：

- feminist · adj. · 女性主义的；义项 sense-staged-ox-f1de25d660f2daab-p11-a-14；来源 ox-f1de25d660f2daab；证据 p11-a-14-s01；词形 feminist。
- inclusion · n. · 包容；参与机会；义项 sense-staged-ox-a8190c3beb4a9985-p11-a-14；来源 ox-a8190c3beb4a9985；证据 p11-a-14-s01；词形 inclusion。
- hierarchy · n. · 等级结构；义项 sense-staged-ox-6111447faf2622ce-p11-a-14；来源 ox-6111447faf2622ce；证据 p11-a-14-s02；词形 hierarchy。
- governance · n. · 管理方式；治理；义项 sense-staged-ox-acdcd49f188495d0-p11-a-14；来源 ox-acdcd49f188495d0；证据 p11-a-14-s03；词形 governance。

审阅者：reviewer-phase11-a；整篇结论：accepted。

读书小组两名成员从固定主持造成参与不均转向自愿轮值提案；最终仅同意列入议程，不冒充全组采纳。标题的拟定及目标的讨论、提出方案各自有准确证据。

## 商定虚构营养课堂材料的表述边界 · p11-a-15

主题：健康与照护；情景：商定虚构营养课堂材料的表述边界（fictional-nutrition-handout-scope）。

沟通目标：区分一般肠道知识与个体症状判断，并商定删除保证改善的说法

背景：两名学员修订虚构营养沟通课程的练习材料；不为任何真实患者提供饮食或诊疗建议。

人物：Sana、Rob。

起草：author-phase11-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p11-a-15-s01 · Sana: The handout says that eating more fiber will cure any gut problem. That sounds too certain.
- p11-a-15-s02 · Rob: It is. The course text discusses fiber as part of a varied diet, not as a cure for every symptom.
- p11-a-15-s03 · Sana: Could we say that the handout gives general information about food and the digestive system?
- p11-a-15-s04 · Rob: Yes. We should also make clear that it cannot explain an individual person’s symptoms.
- p11-a-15-s05 · Sana: I'll remove the promise of a cure and keep that general description.
- p11-a-15-s06 · Rob: Good. We can ask the instructor to check the revised version before it is shared.

目标用法：

- fiber · n. · 膳食纤维；义项 sense-staged-ox-98ed90160a19dac1-p11-a-15；来源 ox-98ed90160a19dac1；证据 p11-a-15-s01；词形 fiber。
- gut · n. · 肠道；义项 sense-staged-ox-73182ec5cab785c9-p11-a-15；来源 ox-73182ec5cab785c9；证据 p11-a-15-s01；词形 gut。

审阅者：reviewer-phase11-a；整篇结论：accepted。

首句过度治愈承诺立即受到质疑和纠正，材料范围被限于一般知识而非个体症状解释；双方同意删承诺并待讲师复核。标题、目标及主张均未将修订或复核当成已经完成。

绑定文件 SHA-256：d200f53b1d3563c6b7853e552ec8d4f98d2f97eee04a965d1924e66677c7cf13

- ox-98ed90160a19dac1：accepted；s01 fiber 指膳食纤维，后文用 varied diet 限定背景；被否定的是万能治愈断言而非词义。（p11-a-15-s01）。
- ox-73182ec5cab785c9：accepted；s01 gut problem 指肠道问题，s03 digestive system 与其语境一致；名词作定语自然。（p11-a-15-s01）。

## 确定梨子收获活动的点心做法 · p11-a-16

主题：饮食沟通；情景：确定梨子收获活动的点心做法（pear-harvest-snack-preparation）。

沟通目标：比较梨的果肉与果皮处理方式，并决定把洗净带皮的梨切片供活动品尝

背景：社区果园收获活动的两位志愿者准备简单的梨子点心；谈论食材特征而非健康治疗。

人物：Poppy、Hugh。

起草：author-phase11-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p11-a-16-s01 · Poppy: We have plenty of pears from this harvest. Shall we peel them for the tasting table?
- p11-a-16-s02 · Hugh: The flesh is soft, and the skins are thin. I would leave the skins on.
- p11-a-16-s03 · Poppy: The recipe card says that keeps more of the fibre in the snack too.
- p11-a-16-s04 · Hugh: Fine, as long as we wash the pears first. Shall we slice them rather than make a puree?
- p11-a-16-s05 · Poppy: Yes. Let's serve washed slices with the skin on, and keep the recipe card beside them.

- harvest · n. · 收获；收成；义项 sense-staged-ox-7d26fc8a40103d8f-p11-a-16；来源 ox-7d26fc8a40103d8f；证据 p11-a-16-s01；词形 harvest。
- flesh · n. · 果肉；义项 sense-staged-ox-d2b53bae936c7a20-p11-a-16；来源 ox-d2b53bae936c7a20；证据 p11-a-16-s02；词形 flesh。
- fibre · n. · 膳食纤维；义项 sense-staged-ox-95dbb26152ce7594-p11-a-16；来源 ox-95dbb26152ce7594；证据 p11-a-16-s03；词形 fibre。

审阅者：reviewer-phase11-a；整篇结论：accepted。

收获梨子的品尝准备包含去皮与否、切片还是果泥两项选择；双方决定洗净带皮切片。果园活动、食材特点、配方卡来源与标题目标一致，未虚构点心已经做完。

- ox-d2b53bae936c7a20：accepted；s02 flesh 指梨的果肉，与 skins 明确对照，不是动物肉义。（p11-a-16-s02）。

## 选择河流纪录片的开场画面 · p11-a-17

主题：文娱与运动；情景：选择河流纪录片的开场画面（river-documentary-opening-choice）。

沟通目标：比较河流素材所呈现的信息，并选定不夸大野生动物活动的开场

背景：纪录片剪辑师与导演为一部本地河流短片选择开场素材。

人物：Zoe、Arun。

起草：author-phase11-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p11-a-17-s01 · Zoe: As the film-maker, what do you want viewers to notice first?
- p11-a-17-s02 · Arun: The river itself. Do we have footage of the water before the boats arrive?
- p11-a-17-s03 · Zoe: Yes. There is also a brief glimpse of an otter near the bank, but the image is blurred.
- p11-a-17-s04 · Arun: I like the imagery of light moving across the water. We do not need the otter to carry the opening.
- p11-a-17-s05 · Zoe: Shall I use the clear river shot and save the otter clip for later, with no claim that otters are always there?
- p11-a-17-s06 · Arun: Exactly. Start with the river and let the animal appear as the brief encounter it was.

## 约定收集扫描仪错误记录后检修 · p11-a-19

主题：消费与服务；情景：约定收集扫描仪错误记录后检修（scanner-fault-service-plan）。

沟通目标：说明扫描仪预览正常但无法保存的故障，并约定发送筛选后的日志供检修

背景：办公室员工与设备服务人员通过电话排查扫描仪问题；尚未确定硬件或软件原因。

人物：Ellis、Farah。

起草：author-phase11-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p11-a-19-s01 · Ellis: The scanner's inability to save files is holding up our work. The preview still looks normal.
- p11-a-19-s02 · Farah: So the preview is functional, but saving fails. Is any warning indicator showing?
- p11-a-19-s03 · Ellis: A red light flashes when I press Save. Is that a hardware fault?
- p11-a-19-s04 · Farah: I can't tell yet. Can you filter the error log by the scanner's device name and send me those entries?
- p11-a-19-s05 · Ellis: Yes. I can send the relevant entries without including the scanned documents.
- p11-a-19-s06 · Farah: Thank you. I'll review them and arrange an inspection; please leave the scanner out of use until then.

## 约定复查租住房间的松动灯具 · p11-a-20

主题：居家生活；情景：约定复查租住房间的松动灯具（rental-light-fixture-snag）。

沟通目标：指出灯具缺陷与验收遗漏，并约定由电工检查后再决定处理

背景：租客和房屋管理员在入住验收时发现灯具问题；他们只安排专业检查，不进行电气维修。

人物：Dina、Leo。

起草：author-phase11-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p11-a-20-s01 · Dina: This light fixture moves when the door closes. There seems to be a flaw in the bracket.
- p11-a-20-s02 · Leo: The inspection report says everything was checked.
- p11-a-20-s03 · Dina: Then the inspection was flawed. It missed something we can see without touching the fitting.
- p11-a-20-s04 · Leo: I could add a note saying we will watch it.
- p11-a-20-s05 · Dina: That would be inadequate. I want an electrician to inspect it before we decide it is safe to use.
- p11-a-20-s06 · Leo: You're right. I'll arrange that and ask you not to use this light in the meantime.
- p11-a-20-s07 · Dina: Thank you. Please let me know the inspection time once you have it.

## 商定容许不同表达方式的团队工作坊 · p11-a-21

主题：工作与协作；情景：商定容许不同表达方式的团队工作坊（team-workshop-speaking-format）。

沟通目标：明确工作坊不以消除分歧为目标，并选择口头与书面并行的参与方式

背景：团队负责人和主持人筹备一次关于工作流程的内部工作坊。

人物：Hana、Russ。

起草：author-phase11-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p11-a-21-s01 · Hana: Can you facilitate the workshop without letting the loudest voices take over?
- p11-a-21-s02 · Russ: Yes. I would like some flexibility in the format, especially for people who need time to think.
- p11-a-21-s03 · Hana: I want to foster a sense that everyone can contribute, not force everyone to agree.
- p11-a-21-s04 · Russ: Exactly. Harmony does not have to mean silence about problems.
- p11-a-21-s05 · Hana: What would you change?
- p11-a-21-s06 · Russ: Let people write down a concern first, then offer a chance to discuss it aloud.
- p11-a-21-s07 · Hana: Let's do that, and let them hand in the note without speaking if they prefer.
- p11-a-21-s08 · Russ: That works. I'll build both options into the session.

- facilitate · v. · 引导；促成；义项 sense-staged-ox-f77c61788695a45d-p11-a-21；来源 ox-f77c61788695a45d；证据 p11-a-21-s01；词形 facilitate。
- flexibility · n. · 灵活性；义项 sense-staged-ox-c624678cdd800a16-p11-a-21；来源 ox-c624678cdd800a16；证据 p11-a-21-s02；词形 flexibility。
- foster · v. · 促进；培养；义项 sense-staged-ox-bcedc9f87ca71a5f-p11-a-21；来源 ox-bcedc9f87ca71a5f；证据 p11-a-21-s03；词形 foster。
- harmony · n. · 和谐；义项 sense-staged-ox-12a35cf06c97f8ae-p11-a-21；来源 ox-12a35cf06c97f8ae；证据 p11-a-21-s04；词形 harmony。

审阅者：reviewer-phase11-a；整篇结论：accepted。

负责人和主持人均反对强迫一致或让大声者主导，最后接受先写、自愿说或只交纸条的具体方式。工作坊尚未举行，标题及目标只确认方案与边界，私有结果状态准确。

## 约定核实花园展板的植物与传统信息 · p11-a-22

主题：自然与环境；情景：约定核实花园展板的植物与传统信息（garden-heritage-panel-review）。

沟通目标：区分原生植物与后引入植物，并约定请相关社群核对传统知识的介绍

背景：花园工作人员依据地方植物目录审阅展板，拟在公开前向当地原住民文化中心核实传统知识说明。

人物：Ari、Helen。

- p11-a-22-s01 · Ari: This panel says all the plants here are indigenous to the valley. Is that right?
- p11-a-22-s02 · Helen: No. The local plant catalogue lists these three as native, but the roses were introduced later.
- p11-a-22-s03 · Ari: The roses certainly flourish here. I suppose that does not prove they came from here.
- p11-a-22-s04 · Helen: Exactly. And the paragraph about cultural heritage needs another check; we wrote it without input from the local Indigenous cultural centre.
- p11-a-22-s05 · Ari: I'll separate the plant origins, then ask the centre whether and how the traditional uses should be described.
- p11-a-22-s06 · Helen: Good. Keep that paragraph off the display until we have their response.

## 帮助新生明确校园求助问题 · p11-a-23

- p11-a-23-s03 · Mateo: No. I'm worried that my ignorance of the system will make me look unprepared.
- p11-a-23-s04 · Wendy: Not knowing a new system is normal. What exactly would you like to ask?
- p11-a-23-s05 · Mateo: Where the booking page is, and whether I can choose a time. Will it be different because I am a foreigner?
- p11-a-23-s06 · Wendy: The desk can check your access, but being from another country is not a reason to avoid asking.
- p11-a-23-s07 · Mateo: All right. I'll ask them to show me the booking page and explain the available times.

## 拟定学校与图书馆合作的名额方案 · p11-a-24

主题：社区与公共空间；情景：拟定学校与图书馆合作的名额方案（library-school-partnership-plan）。

- p11-a-24-s01 · Lotte: We want to forge a partnership with your school, rather than offer one visit and disappear.
- p11-a-24-s02 · Imran: Then we need to formulate a plan the teachers can actually use. How many classes could you host?
- p11-a-24-s03 · Lotte: Two a week. Could we use a simple formula: one visit for each class, spread across the term?
- p11-a-24-s04 · Imran: That would work for our six classes. We could spread them over three weeks to start.
- p11-a-24-s05 · Lotte: Let's put that forward as a trial, then ask teachers whether the timing works.
- p11-a-24-s06 · Imran: Yes. I'll draft the class list for the proposal, without treating those weeks as confirmed bookings.

沟通目标：说明对虚构加盟方案的顾虑，并决定在了解费用前不签署文件

背景：虚构商业沟通练习：两位角色讨论一家编造的咖啡店加盟提案，不构成投资或法律建议。

人物：Rafael、June。

起草：author-phase11-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p11-a-27-s01 · Rafael: Are you ready to sign for the coffee franchise?
- p11-a-27-s02 · June: Frankly, no. The brochure shows the entry fee but says little about the ongoing costs.
- p11-a-27-s03 · Rafael: The representative wants an answer today.
- p11-a-27-s04 · June: I am inclined to wait. I want a written breakdown of the monthly charges before I decide.
- p11-a-27-s05 · Rafael: Then I will tell her you are not signing today and ask for that breakdown.
- p11-a-27-s06 · June: Yes, please. Asking for details is not an agreement to join.

目标用法：

- franchise · n. · 特许经营权；加盟业务；义项 sense-staged-ox-ee1e2af0adc2356a-p11-a-27；来源 ox-ee1e2af0adc2356a；证据 p11-a-27-s01；词形 franchise。
- frankly · adv. · 坦率地说；义项 sense-staged-ox-c073bc2fda3187e1-p11-a-27；来源 ox-c073bc2fda3187e1；证据 p11-a-27-s02；词形 frankly。
- inclined · adj. · 倾向于的；义项 sense-staged-ox-51cffcbdd80df5b7-p11-a-27；来源 ox-51cffcbdd80df5b7；证据 p11-a-27-s04；词形 inclined。

审阅者：reviewer-phase11-a；整篇结论：accepted。

虽然公开文件字节未变，仍完整复读六轮、三项目标和全部当前 outcome evidence。第一项目标解释现为资料列出初始费、对持续费用介绍很少，准确对应 s02 的 says little，不再改写成完全没有信息。s04–s06 支持先索取书面明细、当天不签且询问不等于加盟；决定已作出，而请求发送和签约均未被虚构完成。

## 商定葬礼后的安静聚会与自愿扫墓 · p11-a-28

主题：家庭与人际；情景：商定葬礼后的安静聚会与自愿扫墓（family-memorial-quiet-plan）。

沟通目标：了解家人对葬礼后安排的需要，并约定安静聚会且不强求一起扫墓

背景：两位成年亲属为已去世的姨妈 Nora 商量葬礼当天的家庭安排。

人物：Alice、Dan。

起草：author-phase11-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p11-a-28-s01 · Alice: After Aunt Nora’s funeral, would you like a large gathering at the house?
- p11-a-28-s02 · Dan: I would rather keep it quiet. Grief makes conversation exhausting for me at the moment.
- p11-a-28-s03 · Alice: Then just the close family, with tea and somewhere you can sit alone if you want.
- p11-a-28-s04 · Dan: Thank you. Are people going to visit her grave afterward?
- p11-a-28-s05 · Alice: Some want to, but nobody has to go. We can make that a separate choice.
- p11-a-28-s06 · Dan: That helps. A quiet cup of tea with the family sounds right for me.

目标用法：

- funeral · n. · 葬礼；义项 sense-staged-ox-ad30c5eff532f724-p11-a-28；来源 ox-ad30c5eff532f724；证据 p11-a-28-s01；词形 funeral。
- gathering · n. · 聚会；义项 sense-staged-ox-62f56425bddd0571-p11-a-28；来源 ox-62f56425bddd0571；证据 p11-a-28-s01；词形 gathering。
- grief · n. · 悲痛；义项 sense-staged-ox-2665ee2dc2c41598-p11-a-28；来源 ox-2665ee2dc2c41598；证据 p11-a-28-s02；词形 grief。
- grave · n. · 坟墓；义项 sense-staged-ox-06bb2ec4531a264a-p11-a-28；来源 ox-06bb2ec4531a264a；证据 p11-a-28-s04；词形 grave。

审阅者：reviewer-phase11-a；整篇结论：accepted。

两位成年亲属商量姨妈葬礼后的安排，Dan 表达悲痛及疲惫，Alice 接受小范围喝茶与独处需求，墓地探访保留自愿。标题、目标和主张只称安排达成，未将悲痛等同必须参加活动。

- p11-a-29-s01 · Will: Can I grind a handful of almonds in your coffee grinder for the biscuits?
- p11-a-29-s02 · Esme: Please use the small food processor instead. I do not want bits of almond left in the coffee grinder.
- p11-a-29-s03 · Will: Of course. Is this the processor on the shelf?
- p11-a-29-s04 · Esme: Yes, that one. The clean bowl is beside it.
- p11-a-29-s05 · Will: Found it. I'll use this for the almonds and leave the grinder for the coffee beans.

审阅者：reviewer-phase11-a；整篇结论：accepted。

## 用桌面摆锤模型解释重力 · p11-a-30

主题：学习与技能；情景：用桌面摆锤模型解释重力（classroom-pendulum-demonstration）。

沟通目标：澄清摆锤下降与重力的关系，并约定先放手观察而不是用力推动

背景：科学老师与成年学习者使用低矮桌面上的软球摆锤模型，不使用大型或危险装置。

人物：Nadia、Felix。

起草：author-phase11-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p11-a-30-s01 · Nadia: I still cannot grasp why the ball speeds up as it swings downward.
- p11-a-30-s02 · Felix: Gravity pulls the ball downward, so it gains speed as it moves toward the bottom of its swing. We can watch that on this little model.
- p11-a-30-s03 · Nadia: Should I give it a push?
- p11-a-30-s04 · Felix: Not for this comparison. Keep a light grip on the soft ball, then open your hand without pushing.
- p11-a-30-s05 · Nadia: So we are observing what happens from a release, not adding an extra shove.
- p11-a-30-s06 · Felix: Exactly. Let us watch one swing first, then talk about the change in speed.

目标用法：

- grasp · v. · 理解；义项 sense-staged-ox-3d4455b58f90851f-p11-a-30；来源 ox-3d4455b58f90851f；证据 p11-a-30-s01；词形 grasp。
- gravity · n. · 重力；义项 sense-staged-ox-1ffc968402a5f6e8-p11-a-30；来源 ox-1ffc968402a5f6e8；证据 p11-a-30-s02；词形 gravity。
- grip · n. · 抓握；义项 sense-staged-ox-dcb86bbc4725455c-p11-a-30；来源 ox-dcb86bbc4725455c；证据 p11-a-30-s04；词形 grip。

审阅者：reviewer-phase11-a；整篇结论：accepted。

低矮桌面的软球摆锤给基础解释提供指称，老师说明重力与下摆增速，并区分释放和额外推动。学习者复述方法后双方约定观察，证据没有虚构摆动已执行；标题的解释任务成立。

绑定文件 SHA-256：dda2c8bae9e14f2eca9ee808069adfff934f14ede0e58fdd8c7d9f6f341891ab

- ox-3d4455b58f90851f：accepted；s01 cannot grasp why 指尚未理解原因的动词，与后续解释相连而非抓住实体。（p11-a-30-s01）。
- ox-1ffc968402a5f6e8：accepted；s02 Gravity pulls the ball downward 明确指重力，基础模型中下摆增速的解释一致。（p11-a-30-s02）。
- ox-dcb86bbc4725455c：accepted；s04 a light grip on the soft ball 是轻握的名词用法，与随后张手释放形成自然动作对照。（p11-a-30-s04）。

## 约定修正课堂水力发电模型的说明 · p11-a-31

主题：学习与技能；情景：约定修正课堂水力发电模型的说明（water-power-model-label）。

沟通目标：明确水在模型中的作用与模型未接入电网的边界，并商定说明牌用语

背景：科学课上的小型水轮模型由封闭水槽供水，只能点亮一颗模型灯；学员修订其展示说明。

人物：Quinn、Sofia。

起草：author-phase11-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p11-a-31-s01 · Quinn: Our label says this model supplies power to the national grid. That cannot be right, can it?
- p11-a-31-s02 · Sofia: No. It only lights this tiny lamp. There is no connection to the grid.
- p11-a-31-s03 · Quinn: And the water is the fluid that turns the wheel, rather than a fuel that gets burned?
- p11-a-31-s04 · Sofia: Correct. A real dam may be immense, but this is only a small model of one part of the process.
- p11-a-31-s05 · Quinn: Then the label should say: flowing water turns the wheel to power the model lamp.
- p11-a-31-s06 · Sofia: Yes. Remove the national-grid claim and keep that description.

目标用法：

- grid · n. · 输电网；义项 sense-staged-ox-7bd0c8258744ca0e-p11-a-31；来源 ox-7bd0c8258744ca0e；证据 p11-a-31-s01；词形 grid。
- fluid · n. · 流体；液体；义项 sense-staged-ox-06116e942e31ec5d-p11-a-31；来源 ox-06116e942e31ec5d；证据 p11-a-31-s03；词形 fluid。
- immense · adj. · 巨大的；义项 sense-staged-ox-c3b19552a0486f4a-p11-a-31；来源 ox-c3b19552a0486f4a；证据 p11-a-31-s04；词形 immense。

审阅者：reviewer-phase11-a；整篇结论：accepted。

模型只亮小灯且未接电网，水的推动作用与燃料燃烧被明确区别；双方同意改说明牌但未声称已改。桌面水轮与课堂背景支持题目、情景及所有主张。

- ox-7bd0c8258744ca0e：accepted；s01 national grid 指国家输电网络；后两句纠正连接事实，名词电网义清楚。（p11-a-31-s01）。
- ox-06116e942e31ec5d：accepted；s03 the fluid that turns the wheel 指水这种推动水轮的液体，名词用法自然。（p11-a-31-s03）。

## 约定修正小说中人质场景的人物关系概括 · p11-a-32

主题：文娱与运动；情景：约定修正小说中人质场景的人物关系概括（fictional-hostage-scene-tone）。

沟通目标：根据虚构场景区分敌对态度与友谊，并商定不把食物援助写成和解

背景：读书会成员讨论完全虚构的战争小说一段：一名游击队员看守一名人质，二人的关系仍然紧张。

人物：Yasmin、Theo。

起草：author-phase11-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p11-a-32-s01 · Yasmin: I wrote that the guerrilla and the hostage become friends in this scene.
- p11-a-32-s02 · Theo: Friends? The guard gives him food, but he still refuses to let him contact his family.
- p11-a-32-s03 · Yasmin: True. The hostage sounds hostile when he asks when he can leave.
- p11-a-32-s04 · Theo: And the hostility does not disappear when the meal arrives. Neither of them trusts the other.
- p11-a-32-s05 · Yasmin: I'll say there is a moment of practical help within a relationship that remains tense.
- p11-a-32-s06 · Theo: That is closer. Giving someone food is not the same as releasing them or winning their trust.

- hostage · n. · 人质；义项 sense-staged-ox-2a54b8862c685d56-p11-a-32；来源 ox-2a54b8862c685d56；证据 p11-a-32-s01；词形 hostage。
- hostile · adj. · 敌对的；不友善的；义项 sense-staged-ox-63341c866c0efebc-p11-a-32；来源 ox-63341c866c0efebc；证据 p11-a-32-s03；词形 hostile。
- hostility · n. · 敌意；义项 sense-staged-ox-3971fcb46654318a-p11-a-32；来源 ox-3971fcb46654318a；证据 p11-a-32-s04；词形 hostility。

审阅者：reviewer-phase11-a；整篇结论：accepted。

小说中给食物并未解除监禁、恢复联系或建立信任；读者据此纠正成为朋友的概括，约定写成紧张关系中的帮助。角色关系、反应及元数据均受完整情节支持。

## 商定虚构档案展的幸存者叙事重点 · p11-a-33

主题：社会与公共事务；情景：商定虚构档案展的幸存者叙事重点（fictional-archive-exhibit-framing）。

沟通目标：区分暴行记录与援助经历，并约定以幸存者自己的叙述组织展览

背景：策展课程使用完全虚构的地区和历史档案，档案设定记录一次种族灭绝及其后的援助；不描述现实事件。

人物：Moses、Elin。

起草：author-phase11-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p11-a-33-s01 · Moses: The fictional archive documents a genocide, but our draft exhibition is almost entirely about the officials responsible.
- p11-a-33-s02 · Elin: The survivors need more space. There are accounts of humanitarian work in the camps as well as testimony about the killings.
- p11-a-33-s03 · Moses: What stayed with me was the humanity of the people sharing their food with strangers.
- p11-a-33-s04 · Elin: We can include that without making the suffering seem less serious.
- p11-a-33-s05 · Moses: Let's organize the next draft around the survivors' own accounts, with separate sections for persecution and later assistance.
- p11-a-33-s06 · Elin: Agreed. We should keep the dates and speakers clear so those different experiences do not get blurred together.

- genocide · n. · 种族灭绝；义项 sense-staged-ox-df16e6cc580895af-p11-a-33；来源 ox-df16e6cc580895af；证据 p11-a-33-s01；词形 genocide。

- humanity · n. · 人道；仁慈；义项 sense-staged-ox-4a5f9b99026d69a4-p11-a-33；来源 ox-4a5f9b99026d69a4；证据 p11-a-33-s03；词形 humanity。

审阅者：reviewer-phase11-a；整篇结论：accepted。

完全虚构档案中杀戮证言和后续援助并存；以幸存者自述组织下一稿，不把援助冲淡暴行。标题、目标及证据均保留未来拟稿、日期和叙述者区分，未作现实事件断言。

- ox-df16e6cc580895af：accepted；s01 a genocide 是虚构档案所记录的种族灭绝，名词使用明确且无现实归因。（p11-a-33-s01）。
- ox-cd9a8ac95c207c6b：accepted；s02 humanitarian work in the camps 用形容词修饰营地人道援助工作，后文共享食物使范围具体。（p11-a-33-s02）。
- ox-4a5f9b99026d69a4：accepted；s03 humanity of the people sharing food 指仁慈、人道品质，而非全人类的另一名词义。（p11-a-33-s03）。

<a id="dialogue-p11-a-34"></a>

## 商定观日出后的出租车返程 · p11-a-34

主题：出行与住宿；情景：商定观日出后的出租车返程（sunrise-return-taxi-plan）。

沟通目标：确认观景结束后的交通需要，并决定步行到出租车站等车

背景：两位朋友在城市海滨看完日出，讨论如何返回住处；没有预订车辆。

人物：Paula、Jin。

起草：author-phase11-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p11-a-34-s01 · Paula: That was a glorious sunrise. The whole horizon turned pink.

- p11-a-36-s04 · Mina: Then the card is wrong. The release happens in the final act, after the year has passed.

- p11-a-37-s04 · Petra: Exactly. Can you try the summary again with that distinction?
- p11-a-37-s05 · Eli: One councillor proposes a prison penalty, but the city has not adopted it and currently uses warnings and fines.

## 商定虚构噪声数据的谨慎因果表述 · p11-a-38

主题：研究与论证；情景：商定虚构噪声数据的谨慎因果表述（classroom-noise-data-caution）。

沟通目标：区分头痛报告发生率与因果结论，并商定保留不确定性的报告表述

背景：数据素养课堂使用教师编造的噪声与头痛问卷结果，只练习证据表达，不构成健康判断。

人物：Freya、Anton。

起草：author-phase11-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p11-a-38-s01 · Freya: In this practice dataset, the incidence of reported headaches is higher on noisy shifts.
- p11-a-38-s02 · Anton: Does that prove noise caused the headaches?
- p11-a-38-s03 · Freya: No. The questionnaire does not control for hours worked or lack of sleep.
- p11-a-38-s04 · Anton: Then saying it proves harm would heighten concern beyond what these data support.
- p11-a-38-s05 · Freya: I will write that the reports occur more often on noisy shifts, but the reason is not established.
- p11-a-38-s06 · Anton: Good. Include the missing information about working hours and sleep too.

目标用法：

- incidence · n. · 发生率；义项 sense-staged-ox-aa6301231b22a97a-p11-a-38；来源 ox-aa6301231b22a97a；证据 p11-a-38-s01；词形 incidence。
- heighten · v. · 加剧；增强；义项 sense-staged-ox-5ee131d83b050ed1-p11-a-38；来源 ox-5ee131d83b050ed1；证据 p11-a-38-s04；词形 heighten。

审阅者：reviewer-phase11-a；整篇结论：accepted。

练习问卷只能显示噪声班次的头痛报告差异，工时及睡眠因素未控制，末句要求注明缺失信息。双方同意谨慎报告而非作健康因果断言，标题、目标和主张均保留不确定性。

- ox-5ee131d83b050ed1：accepted；s04 heighten concern 指夸大的因果表述会加剧担忧，动词搭配及条件语气自然。（p11-a-38-s04）。

- p11-a-39-s03 · Nell: But later he remembers their childhood with affection. Being troubled by a memory is not the same as hating someone.
- p11-a-39-s04 · Hugo: You are right. I will introduce it as a poem about anger mixed with affection and regret.
- p11-a-39-s05 · Nell: That gives the audience room to hear the different feelings in the poem.

## 确认模型注射课先观察再练习 · p11-b-01

主题：健康与照护；情景：确认模型注射课先观察再练习（mannequin-injection-demonstration-order）。

沟通目标：明确示范对象是训练模型，并约定先看示范再接受教师指导

背景：虚构护理模拟课堂。学生和教师确认课程顺序，不涉及对真人进行注射或具体操作方法。

人物：Nadia、Felix。

起草：author-phase11-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p11-b-01-s01 · Nadia: Is the injection practice happening today? I haven't used this training arm before.
- p11-b-01-s02 · Felix: Yes, but this first session is a demonstration. We won't inject anything into a person.
- p11-b-01-s03 · Nadia: Will we be able to see the point of insertion from the back row?
- p11-b-01-s04 · Felix: There's a camera above the model, and the image will appear on that screen.
- p11-b-01-s05 · Nadia: So I should watch first rather than try to copy you at the same time?
- p11-b-01-s06 · Felix: Exactly. After the demonstration, I'll instruct each student individually using the model.
- p11-b-01-s07 · Nadia: All right. I'll leave the practice kit closed until it's my turn with you.

目标用法：

- inject · v. · 给……注射；义项 sense-staged-ox-3d790011fecfdc95-p11-b-01；来源 ox-3d790011fecfdc95；证据 p11-b-01-s02；词形 inject。
- injection · n. · 注射；义项 sense-staged-ox-e2379442bbf28bf3-p11-b-01；来源 ox-e2379442bbf28bf3；证据 p11-b-01-s01；词形 injection。
- insertion · n. · 插入；义项 sense-staged-ox-c376e69f24b6d6c6-p11-b-01；来源 ox-c376e69f24b6d6c6；证据 p11-b-01-s03；词形 insertion。
- instruct · v. · 指导、教授操作；义项 sense-staged-ox-c21c5b746d8e6da3-p11-b-01；来源 ox-c21c5b746d8e6da3；证据 p11-b-01-s06；词形 instruct。

审阅者：reviewer-phase11-b；整篇结论：accepted。

The simulated classroom consistently distinguishes the training arm from a person. Nadia confirms observation before guided practice and accepts Felix’s sequence in s05–s07; title, scene and outcome claims describe that agreement, not a completed injection.

- ox-3d790011fecfdc95：accepted；s02 uses the base verb after will not, with something injected into a person; the negative practice boundary still clearly demonstrates the injection sense.（p11-b-01-s02）。
- ox-e2379442bbf28bf3：accepted；s01 uses the noun attributively in injection practice, matching the model-based nursing lesson.（p11-b-01-s01）。
- ox-c376e69f24b6d6c6：accepted；s03 refers to the point where the needle enters the model; point of insertion naturally supplies the insertion noun sense.（p11-b-01-s03）。

## 把婴儿探视改为视频通话 · p11-b-02

主题：健康与照护；情景：把婴儿探视改为视频通话（fictional-infant-visit-video-alternative）。

沟通目标：说明访客担心咳嗽带来的感染风险，并约定改用视频探视

背景：虚构病房探视沟通。访客向接待员说明自己的顾虑，选择不进入病房；不提供诊断或个人治疗建议。

人物：Owen、Mina。

起草：author-phase11-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p11-b-02-s01 · Owen: I've come to visit my sister and her infant, but I've developed a cough.
- p11-b-02-s02 · Mina: Thank you for telling me before going into the ward.

## 试行先匿名写意见再自愿讨论 · p11-b-04

主题：工作与协作；情景：试行先匿名写意见再自愿讨论（quiet-team-feedback-channel-trial）。

- p11-b-04-s03 · Ada: I don't think silence is an inherent feature of this team. People send me useful comments afterwards.
- p11-b-04-s04 · Marcus: Then let's give everyone a private way to contribute before we meet. Their involvement shouldn't depend on enjoying a debate.
- p11-b-04-s05 · Ada: Would anonymous written comments followed by an optional discussion work?
- p11-b-04-s06 · Marcus: Yes. Ask for one specific problem and one possible change, so the feedback is meaningful rather than just a score.
- p11-b-04-s07 · Ada: Let's try that next week, without the public ranking, and ask people how the format felt.

- involvement · n. · 参与；义项 sense-staged-ox-20328c9abb926f10-p11-b-04；来源 ox-20328c9abb926f10；证据 p11-b-04-s04；词形 involvement。
- meaningful · adj. · 有实际意义的；义项 sense-staged-ox-80694d2496c92712-p11-b-04；来源 ox-80694d2496c92712；证据 p11-b-04-s06；词形 meaningful。

审阅者：reviewer-phase11-b；整篇结论：accepted。

The managers treat ranking as a possible inhibitor rather than proven causation. Marcus accepts anonymous written feedback and optional discussion in s06, and Ada schedules a trial, so the title and goal accurately describe an agreed experiment, not measured improvement.

主题：工作与协作；情景：因检查记录缺失暂停使用工作坊机器（workshop-machine-preuse-inspection）。

背景：工作坊负责人和新任值班人员在开门前核对机器记录，不涉及机器维修操作。

- p11-b-09-s06 · Amir: Helen is on the duty list. I'll ask her to come over and complete the check.
- p11-b-09-s07 · Tessa: I'll keep the machine marked unavailable until she confirms it's ready.
- p11-b-09-s08 · Amir: Good. We'll direct the first group to the drawing tables meanwhile.

## 约定测试柔和渐变的幻想短片结尾 · p11-b-11

主题：文娱与运动；情景：约定测试柔和渐变的幻想短片结尾（fantasy-film-brightness-edit-test）。

沟通目标：说明试看片时对强光片段的不适感受，并约定不再增强闪光、改试柔和渐变

背景：两名剪辑师在虚构幻想短片试看片后讨论视觉效果；健康感受只是角色反馈，不构成诊疗建议。

人物：Silas、Mei。

起草：author-phase11-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p11-b-11-s01 · Silas: The forest looks magical, but I felt sick during the rapid flashes at the end.
- p11-b-11-s02 · Mei: I was going to intensify the flashes as the door opened. That may be the wrong direction.
- p11-b-11-s03 · Silas: I'd rather not use an effect that could induce nausea. Can we keep the colour without the flashing?
- p11-b-11-s04 · Mei: We could lower the intensity of the light and use a slow fade from blue to gold.
- p11-b-11-s05 · Silas: That sounds worth trying. The door can still feel surprising without the screen pulsing.
- p11-b-11-s06 · Mei: I'll make that version, without the flashes, and we'll watch it before choosing the final ending.
- p11-b-11-s07 · Silas: Agreed. Let's test the gentler fade first.

目标用法：

- induce · v. · 引起、诱发；义项 sense-staged-ox-a583fbe21a93911b-p11-b-11；来源 ox-a583fbe21a93911b；证据 p11-b-11-s03；词形 induce。
- intensify · v. · 增强、加剧；义项 sense-staged-ox-5f76df5f46c5a920-p11-b-11；来源 ox-5f76df5f46c5a920；证据 p11-b-11-s02；词形 intensify。
- intensity · n. · 强度；义项 sense-staged-ox-c76b5828e2835fd7-p11-b-11；来源 ox-c76b5828e2835fd7；证据 p11-b-11-s04；词形 intensity。
- magical · adj. · 奇妙迷人的、具有魔幻感的；义项 sense-staged-ox-55497d107f7aa4c0-p11-b-11；来源 ox-55497d107f7aa4c0；证据 p11-b-11-s01；词形 magical。

审阅者：reviewer-phase11-b；整篇结论：accepted。

The editors respond to a reported unpleasant viewing experience by agreeing to test a gentler, flash-free fade. They do not diagnose the viewer or claim a final edit has already been selected; the title/goal and evidence accurately distinguish this test agreement.

## 为中级写作密集课准备短篇摘录 · p11-b-12

主题：学习与技能；情景：为中级写作密集课准备短篇摘录（writing-course-manuscript-preparation）。

- p11-b-12-s01 · Gabriel: I've enrolled in the intensive writing course. Does intensive mean we'll write all day?
- p11-b-12-s02 · Suki: There are two workshops each morning and a short assignment for the afternoon. It's aimed at intermediate writers, not complete beginners.
- p11-b-12-s03 · Gabriel: I've taken a term of classes, but my manuscript is unfinished. Is that a problem?
- p11-b-12-s04 · Suki: No. We need two pages from an original story, not a complete book.
- p11-b-12-s05 · Gabriel: My opening includes a lengthy account of the village. Would a scene with two characters be more useful?
- p11-b-12-s06 · Suki: Yes, because the first workshop is about dialogue. Choose two pages where the characters want different things.
- p11-b-12-s07 · Gabriel: I'll bring the argument scene from my story, then. It's just under two pages.
- p11-b-12-s08 · Suki: That will work.

- intensive · adj. · 密集的；义项 sense-staged-ox-f609660c5c8f0b92-p11-b-12；来源 ox-f609660c5c8f0b92；证据 p11-b-12-s01；词形 intensive。

- manuscript · n. · 手稿、未出版的文稿；义项 sense-staged-ox-1c376c56e821cb0c-p11-b-12；来源 ox-1c376c56e821cb0c；证据 p11-b-12-s03；词形 manuscript。
- lengthy · adj. · 冗长的；义项 sense-staged-ox-e04c8fd7d125b7fd-p11-b-12；来源 ox-e04c8fd7d125b7fd；证据 p11-b-12-s05；词形 lengthy。

审阅者：reviewer-phase11-b；整篇结论：accepted。

The coordinator explains the intensive course schedule and intermediate entry level, then confirms a short original dialogue excerpt is sufficient. Gabriel selects an argument scene and Suki accepts it; preparation is agreed, not reported as completed attendance.

## 逐项测试并记录录音背景噪声 · p11-b-13

主题：工作与协作；情景：逐项测试并记录录音背景噪声（studio-hum-isolation-test）。

沟通目标：区分观察到的噪声与尚未确定的原因，并约定单独改变设备状态后记录比较结果

背景：录音室工作人员检查一段八秒试录中的嗡嗡声，尚未确定原因。

人物：Ren、Paula。

起草：author-phase11-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p11-b-13-s01 · Ren: There's a hum in this test recording. It starts just as the printer begins running.
- p11-b-13-s02 · Paula: It could be interference, but the timing alone doesn't prove where it's coming from.
- p11-b-13-s03 · Ren: I'll play the eight-second section on a loop so we can hear the change.
- p11-b-13-s04 · Paula: Then let's make a fresh recording with the printer switched off and everything else unchanged.
- p11-b-13-s05 · Ren: Should I also move the microphone?
- p11-b-13-s06 · Paula: Not in the same test. That would interfere with the comparison. Let's change one thing at a time.
- p11-b-13-s07 · Ren: Understood. I'll log the equipment settings and the result for each recording.
- p11-b-13-s08 · Paula: Good. We'll compare the first pair before deciding what to test next.

目标用法：

- interfere · v. · 干扰、妨碍；义项 sense-staged-ox-4187012ba4e12622-p11-b-13；来源 ox-4187012ba4e12622；证据 p11-b-13-s06；词形 interfere。
- interference · n. · 电磁或信号干扰；义项 sense-staged-ox-1aa08cdf28b02f08-p11-b-13；来源 ox-1aa08cdf28b02f08；证据 p11-b-13-s02；词形 interference。
- loop · n. · 循环播放的片段；义项 sense-staged-ox-133afd243ad290f4-p11-b-13；来源 ox-133afd243ad290f4；证据 p11-b-13-s03；词形 loop。
- log · v. · 记录；义项 sense-staged-ox-48633d32a327aac6-p11-b-13；来源 ox-48633d32a327aac6；证据 p11-b-13-s07；词形 log。

审阅者：reviewer-phase11-b；整篇结论：accepted。

The studio discussion avoids treating simultaneous printer activity as proven cause. They agree to change one variable, log settings and compare fresh recordings before deciding the next test; metadata and evidence correctly mark those future operations as agreed.

## 确认渡轮到大陆后的步行路线 · p11-b-15

主题：出行与住宿；情景：确认渡轮到大陆后的步行路线（mainland-ferry-walking-route）。

沟通目标：辨认去游客中心途中两个不同路口，并用钟楼作为第二次转弯的地标

背景：旅客在虚构海岛渡轮的服务台询问抵达大陆码头后步行去游客中心的路线。

人物：Elena、Scott。

起草：author-phase11-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p11-b-15-s01 · Elena: When the ferry reaches the mainland, can I walk from the pier to the visitor centre?
- p11-b-15-s02 · Scott: Yes. Follow Pier Street to the intersection with King Street and turn left.
- p11-b-15-s03 · Elena: Is that where the clock tower is? I can see it on this map.
- p11-b-15-s04 · Scott: No, the tower is at the next junction, where King Street meets Coast Road. It's a useful landmark for your second turn.
- p11-b-15-s05 · Elena: So I turn left onto King Street first, then head towards the tower?
- p11-b-15-s06 · Scott: Exactly. Turn right at the tower. The visitor centre is the first building on that side.
- p11-b-15-s07 · Elena: Got it: left at King Street, right at the clock tower. I'll keep this map with me.

- p11-b-16-s05 · Mabel: Can we keep the room for twenty minutes after the talk instead of clearing it straight away?
- p11-b-16-s06 · Theo: Yes, our booking allows that. I'll tell the room volunteer not to start packing up until then.
- p11-b-16-s07 · Mabel: Great. Reading room, with twenty minutes for conversation at the end.

- p11-b-17-s05 · Anika: Let's try the new arrangement on one row. We can measure the space and see whether picking orders becomes awkward.

## 保留短剧的讽刺结尾并删去无关段落 · p11-b-19

主题：学习与技能；情景：保留短剧的讽刺结尾并删去无关段落（play-irony-ending-edit）。

沟通目标：解释人物结局与主张的反差，并约定保留该结尾、删除无关家族背景

背景：学生向写作教师征求原创短剧结尾的修改意见，人物和事件均为虚构。

人物：Petra、Miles。

起草：author-phase11-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p11-b-19-s01 · Petra: Is it too obvious that the mayor, who bans all music, ends up asking the band for help?
- p11-b-19-s02 · Miles: No. It's ironic that the person who wanted silence needs the musicians to bring people together.
- p11-b-19-s03 · Petra: Ironically, the ban also made the band more popular. I was thinking of adding a speech explaining that.
- p11-b-19-s04 · Miles: The audience can see the irony already. Let the mayor quietly ask for one song instead.
- p11-b-19-s05 · Petra: What about the page describing his grandfather's bakery?
- p11-b-19-s06 · Miles: It's irrelevant to this ending. Nothing in the scene depends on the bakery.
- p11-b-19-s07 · Petra: Then I'll cut that page and keep the request for a song. The audience can make the connection.

审阅者：reviewer-phase11-b；整篇结论：accepted。

The student and teacher discuss a concrete reversal: a music-banning mayor needs musicians and the ban boosts their popularity. Petra accepts the quiet ending and plans to remove unrelated bakery background; the evidence records an edit agreement, not an already changed manuscript.

- p11-b-20-s09 · Walter: Thursday at ten. I'll start with that conversation.

## 修正虚构庭审记录中的裁决状态 · p11-b-21

主题：学习与技能；情景：修正虚构庭审记录中的裁决状态（fictional-court-record-role-check）。

- p11-b-21-s03 · Helena: And what did the magistrate do?
- p11-b-21-s04 · Isaac: Requested the delivery records. The card says there's no ruling on the complaint yet.
- p11-b-21-s05 · Helena: Then the judicial hearing hasn't settled whether his account is correct. We need to separate his statement from the court's response.
- p11-b-21-s06 · Isaac: I've changed the sentence: 'After hearing the inmate's complaint about undelivered letters, the magistrate requested records; no ruling on the complaint is recorded.'
- p11-b-21-s07 · Helena: That matches the card. Keep that version.

Isaac actually supplies a replacement sentence and Helena approves it, supporting completed correction rather than a mere promise; all legal facts stay within the fictional case card.

## 约定保留虚构诉讼的两项未决问题 · p11-b-22

主题：学习与技能；情景：约定保留虚构诉讼的两项未决问题（fictional-lawsuit-unresolved-scope）。

沟通目标：区分法院管辖争议与责任认定，并约定摘要分别说明两者尚未解决

背景：两位学生讨论虚构民事案件练习材料，只核对给定信息，不推断真实法律结论。

人物：Nell、Arun。

起草：author-phase11-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p11-b-22-s01 · Nell: Can I say the company is liable now that the lawsuit has been filed?
- p11-b-22-s02 · Arun: No. Filing the case isn't the same as deciding it. What else does our fictional case sheet say?
- p11-b-22-s03 · Nell: The company disputes the court's jurisdiction, and the court hasn't answered that challenge.
- p11-b-22-s04 · Arun: Does it record any decision about responsibility for the damage?
- p11-b-22-s05 · Nell: No, it says that question is still unresolved too.
- p11-b-22-s06 · Arun: Then give them separate lines: jurisdiction disputed, and liability not decided.

## 把出席人数预测标为待验证的估计 · p11-b-28

主题：研究与论证；情景：把出席人数预测标为待验证的估计（attendance-model-limit-wording）。

沟通目标：指出线性模型不能直接支持给定概率，并约定说明样本有限、在后续活动检验预测

背景：活动团队讨论只用三场历史活动数据建立的出席人数估计，不涉及真实投资或高风险决策。

人物：Mira、Anton。

起草：author-phase11-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p11-b-28-s01 · Mira: Your mathematical model predicts eighty attendees from a hundred registrations. Can we put that in the planning note?
- p11-b-28-s02 · Anton: As a rough estimate, yes. It's a linear model fitted to just three past events.
- p11-b-28-s03 · Mira: What's the logic behind using registrations?
- p11-b-28-s04 · Anton: More registrations went with higher attendance in those events. But that pattern may not hold for a different kind of event.
- p11-b-28-s05 · Mira: Could we say there's a ninety percent likelihood that at least eighty people will come?
- p11-b-28-s06 · Anton: No. We haven't estimated that probability. The line gives a prediction, not that level of certainty.
- p11-b-28-s07 · Mira: Then I'll label eighty as a rough estimate based on three events, and we'll compare it with actual attendance afterwards.
- p11-b-28-s08 · Anton: Good. That makes the limitation visible instead of hiding it in the model.

审阅者：reviewer-phase11-b；整篇结论：accepted。

The team correctly distinguishes a point prediction based on three events from an unestimated ninety-percent probability. They agree to label the estimate and compare it with later attendance; the goal’s claim concerns this model’s unsupported certainty, not a universal ban on probabilistic linear models.

## 先索取会堂维修分项报价再决定活动规模 · p11-b-30

主题：社区与公共空间；情景：先索取会堂维修分项报价再决定活动规模（hall-repair-budget-risk-query）。

沟通目标：说明维修费用可能影响活动预算，并约定先取得分项报价再作缩减决定

背景：社区会堂筹备人员面对尚未正式报价的维修事项，讨论活动预算安排。

人物：Greta、Leon。

起草：author-phase11-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p11-b-30-s01 · Greta: The repair costs loom over every decision about the autumn event. Are we going to have to cancel it?
- p11-b-30-s02 · Leon: We don't know the magnitude of the bill yet. The caretaker gave us a rough figure, not a written quote.
- p11-b-30-s03 · Greta: Could some of the work wait until after the event?
- p11-b-30-s04 · Leon: We need the contractor to identify what's required before the hall can be used. Let's not assume any of it can wait.
- p11-b-30-s05 · Greta: I'll ask for an itemized quote and a clear distinction between work needed for use and optional improvements.
- p11-b-30-s06 · Leon: Good. Then we can decide whether a smaller event is possible, rather than canceling on the basis of a guess.

目标用法：

- loom · v. · 令人担忧地逼近或笼罩；义项 sense-staged-ox-5919bf7475b27cdd-p11-b-30；来源 ox-5919bf7475b27cdd；证据 p11-b-30-s01；词形 loom。
- magnitude · n. · 规模、程度；义项 sense-staged-ox-47066d0f34eba32a-p11-b-30；来源 ox-47066d0f34eba32a；证据 p11-b-30-s02；词形 magnitude。

审阅者：reviewer-phase11-b；整篇结论：accepted。

The hall planners treat the caretaker’s figure as provisional and will seek an itemized contractor quote, separating required work from optional improvements. They defer the event-scale decision rather than assume repairs can wait or cancel on a guess; the goal and evidence preserve those limits.

## 约定无障碍游行聚焦设施诉求并先询问参与需求 · p11-b-33

## 把虚构解放事件与平民遇害分开展示 · p11-b-34

主题：学习与技能；情景：把虚构解放事件与平民遇害分开展示（fictional-memorial-panel-separation）。

沟通目标：核对虚构时间线中的两个事件，并约定保留自由主题同时单列平民受害情况

背景：学生制作架空国家的纪念展板，练习在叙述中兼顾不同经历；事件不对应真实历史。

人物：Luca、Yara。

起草：author-phase11-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p11-b-34-s01 · Luca: The first panel celebrates liberation on the third of May. Where should we put the events of the tenth?
- p11-b-34-s02 · Yara: The fictional timeline records a massacre of civilians on the tenth. That shouldn't disappear into a general story of victory.
- p11-b-34-s03 · Luca: I wanted the exhibition to be about liberty, but you're right that the people killed didn't get to enjoy it.
- p11-b-34-s04 · Yara: We can keep that theme while giving the civilian victims a separate panel. The dates need to remain distinct.
- p11-b-34-s05 · Luca: Then the first panel can explain the end of occupation, and the second can describe the killings a week later.
- p11-b-34-s06 · Yara: Yes. Let's give the second panel its own space rather than reduce it to a note under the celebration.

目标用法：

- liberation · n. · 解放、摆脱占领；义项 sense-staged-ox-f543295abc9cf421-p11-b-34；来源 ox-f543295abc9cf421；证据 p11-b-34-s01；词形 liberation。

审阅者：reviewer-phase11-b；整篇结论：accepted。

The invented timeline distinguishes liberation on May third from civilian killings on May tenth. Both agree to separate the panels while retaining the freedom theme; neither the title nor the evidence claims the exhibition is already constructed or presents these events as actual history.

- p11-b-36-s01 · Gareth: Are you the lad who emailed about our beginner session? I'm Gareth.

- p11-b-36-s07 · Gareth: Good. There's no need to match anyone else's pace.

## 了解冥想课形式并选定靠门座位 · p11-b-37

主题：文娱与运动；情景：了解冥想课形式并选定靠门座位（meditation-class-format-query）。

沟通目标：说明静坐时的紧张表现，确认课程允许换姿势或离席，并选择靠门开始体验

背景：虚构休闲课程场景。首次参加者在冥想体验课开始前询问教师，不涉及诊断或疗效承诺。

人物：Dina、Rowan。

起草：author-phase11-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p11-b-37-s01 · Dina: This is my first meditation class. Do we have to sit completely still for the whole hour?
- p11-b-37-s02 · Rowan: No. There are short seated periods with breaks, and you can change position or step outside.
- p11-b-37-s03 · Dina: My nerves tend to manifest as a need to keep moving. I don't want to distract everyone.
- p11-b-37-s04 · Rowan: You're welcome to sit near the door. You don't have to explain if you need a break.
- p11-b-37-s05 · Dina: I'll start there, then. Knowing I can step out makes it easier to give the class a try.
- p11-b-37-s06 · Rowan: That seat is free. Take your time getting settled.

目标用法：

- meditation · n. · 冥想、静思练习；义项 sense-staged-ox-52c803d006f909e5-p11-b-37；来源 ox-52c803d006f909e5；证据 p11-b-37-s01；词形 meditation。

审阅者：reviewer-phase11-b；整篇结论：accepted。

- p11-c-07-s02 · Amal: But the story ends with the judge showing mercy to someone who broke the rule. That seems worth discussing.
- p11-c-07-s03 · Theo: Should our question simply be whether the judge was right?
- p11-c-07-s04 · Amal: That could work, but I’d like to connect it to morality: when, if ever, should compassion outweigh a rule?
- p11-c-07-s05 · Theo: That gives us a philosophical question rather than a vote on whether we like the character.
- p11-c-07-s06 · Amal: Let’s use it, and ask people to explain their reasons before we discuss our own views.

## 商定为试办保留两小时准备时间 · p11-c-08

主题：工作与协作；情景：商定为试办保留两小时准备时间（trial-proposal-protected-hours）。

沟通目标：澄清试办准备不能挤入已满日程或要求加班，并同意调整任务保留两小时

背景：两位同事评估一个小型客户接待试办方案。

人物：Hana、Ben。

起草：author-phase11-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p11-c-08-s01 · Hana: There’s merit in trying a shorter welcome session, but who will prepare it?
- p11-c-08-s02 · Ben: I was hoping you could. It’s a mere two hours of preparation.
- p11-c-08-s03 · Hana: Two hours still have to come from somewhere. My objection is to fitting it around a full schedule, not to the idea itself.
- p11-c-08-s04 · Ben: Fair point. I was merely assuming you could fit those two hours in. I should have checked your schedule. You shouldn’t have to work late.
- p11-c-08-s05 · Hana: Could we move Tuesday’s filing task and protect those two hours?
- p11-c-08-s06 · Ben: Yes. I’ll move that task to my list and block Tuesday morning for your preparation.
- p11-c-08-s07 · Hana: Then I’m happy to run the trial session.

- merit · n. · 优点；价值；义项 sense-staged-ox-9d5744d5c139d03b-p11-c-08；来源 ox-9d5744d5c139d03b；证据 p11-c-08-s01；词形 merit。
- mere · adj. · 仅仅的；只不过的；义项 sense-staged-ox-d50ae5dccab4a824-p11-c-08；来源 ox-d50ae5dccab4a824；证据 p11-c-08-s02；词形 mere。

- merely · adv. · 仅仅；只是；义项 sense-staged-ox-9a29d3a63bfee987-p11-c-08；来源 ox-9a29d3a63bfee987；证据 p11-c-08-s04；词形 merely。

审阅者：reviewer-phase11-c；整篇结论：accepted。

Fresh full reread of all seven turns, four targets, scenario and current outcome evidence: Ben now admits assuming the already-estimated two preparation hours would fit Hana's schedule, so s04 no longer contradicts s02. Hana's concern is the full schedule, not opposition to the trial; Ben takes the filing task and reserves Tuesday morning, and she accepts. Title/label/goal and every evidence clause correctly distinguish this agreement from actual calendar changes.

## 约定分开记录部分答卷与完整答卷 · p11-c-09

主题：研究与论证；情景：约定分开记录部分答卷与完整答卷（survey-partial-response-method）。

沟通目标：说明未完成答卷的统计边界，并约定在方法说明中写明纳入条件

背景：研究方法课的两位学员讨论虚构问卷练习的数据处理。

人物：Iris、Malik。

起草：author-phase11-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p11-c-09-s01 · Iris: Twenty people only partially completed the survey. Should we discard those responses?
- p11-c-09-s02 · Malik: Not automatically. A partial response may still answer our first question, but it can’t tell us about the unanswered items.
- p11-c-09-s03 · Iris: Then we shouldn’t overlook the different number of answers to each question.
- p11-c-09-s04 · Malik: Right. One parameter of our analysis is whether the question was actually answered. We’ll report the count for every item.
- p11-c-09-s05 · Iris: I’ll describe that rule in the methodology section and keep partial and complete responses separate in the summary.
- p11-c-09-s06 · Malik: Good. That will make it clear which results use which answers.

- partially · adv. · 部分地；义项 sense-staged-ox-b01e0f4a63c8cb38-p11-c-09；来源 ox-b01e0f4a63c8cb38；证据 p11-c-09-s01；词形 partially。
- partial · adj. · 不完整的；部分的；义项 sense-staged-ox-73f89c459148e55e-p11-c-09；来源 ox-73f89c459148e55e；证据 p11-c-09-s02；词形 partial。
- overlook · v. · 忽略；义项 sense-staged-ox-626aec5db243ded0-p11-c-09；来源 ox-626aec5db243ded0；证据 p11-c-09-s03；词形 overlook。

- methodology · n. · 研究方法体系；义项 sense-staged-ox-a0862097cfb70805-p11-c-09；来源 ox-a0862097cfb70805；证据 p11-c-09-s05；词形 methodology。

审阅者：reviewer-phase11-c；整篇结论：accepted。

The fictional survey discussion preserves per-item evidence boundaries rather than discarding all incomplete responses or treating blanks as answers. Both speakers agree to document inclusion rules and separate partial from complete responses. Title, goal and private claims correctly describe future reporting, not completed analysis.

- p11-c-11-s04 · Owen: We can’t tell from here. They may simply be out of sight; let’s record what we actually see.
- p11-c-11-s05 · Sylvia: All right. I’ll note one adult by the reeds, not an empty nest.
- p11-c-11-s06 · Owen: Good. We’ll use the screen and leave the birds undisturbed.

## 约定暂放反复修改的照片 · p11-c-16

- p11-c-28-s01 · Bruno: I’d like to nominate Jo for the community service award. She’s kept the evening sessions running all year.
- p11-c-28-s02 · Hazel: Does the nominee have to agree first? Jo doesn’t always like being in the spotlight.
- p11-c-28-s03 · Bruno: Yes. The nomination form asks for her consent, so we can’t just surprise her with it.
- p11-c-28-s04 · Hazel: I know her well. I can ask privately whether she’d be comfortable with it.
- p11-c-28-s05 · Bruno: Please do. If she agrees, I’ll collect the session records and write the explanation.
- p11-c-28-s06 · Hazel: And if she says no, we’ll leave it there.
- p11-c-28-s07 · Bruno: Absolutely. Let’s ask her before we prepare the application.

审阅者：reviewer-phase11-c；整篇结论：accepted。

The application form supplies the consent requirement, Hazel agrees to ask Jo privately, and Bruno will prepare records only if she consents. The participants also accept stopping if she refuses. Title, label and every outcome claim accurately describe this conditional plan, not consent or nomination already secured.

## 约定书面确认展示柜借期延长的例外条件 · p11-c-29

主题：消费与服务；情景：约定书面确认展示柜借期延长的例外条件（display-case-loan-extension-conditions）。

沟通目标：核对借用展示柜的归还期限，并约定在书面延期确认前仍按原期限安排归还

背景：虚构地方展览的组织者向器材出借员询问能否多借一天展示柜，纯属服务沟通练习。

人物：Gareth、Anika。

起草：author-phase11-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p11-c-29-s01 · Gareth: Does our loan agreement oblige us to return the display case on Friday, even if the exhibition runs late?
- p11-c-29-s02 · Anika: Yes. That’s the agreed date. I may be able to allow one extra day, but I need to check the next booking.
- p11-c-29-s03 · Gareth: Would that set a precedent for future exhibitions? We don’t want anyone to assume extensions are automatic.
- p11-c-29-s04 · Anika: No. If Saturday is free, I’ll send a written exception for this loan only, with a new return time.
- p11-c-29-s05 · Gareth: Then we’ll keep Friday’s return arrangements unless we receive that confirmation.
- p11-c-29-s06 · Anika: That’s right. I’ll check the booking now, but please don’t change your transport yet.

目标用法：

- oblige · v. · 使负有义务；要求；义项 sense-staged-ox-173718aecd0a6a75-p11-c-29；来源 ox-173718aecd0a6a75；证据 p11-c-29-s01；词形 oblige。
- precedent · n. · 先例；义项 sense-staged-ox-a081d38f91942e0d-p11-c-29；来源 ox-a081d38f91942e0d；证据 p11-c-29-s03；词形 precedent。

审阅者：reviewer-phase11-c；整篇结论：accepted。

The object is explicitly a borrowed display case, not the exhibit itself. Friday remains binding within this fictional agreement; a Saturday extension is conditional on availability and written confirmation. The title's agreement about exception conditions and the goal's unchanged return arrangements are fully supported without granting an extension prematurely.

- p11-c-30-s01 · Zoe: Could I speak to a general practitioner about a persistent ringing in my left ear?
- p11-c-30-s02 · Receptionist Patel: I can pass on a message. When did you first notice it, and is it happening now?
- p11-c-30-s03 · Zoe: The first occurrence was on Monday evening. It has come back each day, and I can hear it now.
- p11-c-30-s04 · Receptionist Patel: I’ll include that in the message and ask a clinician to call you today. I can’t assess the cause myself.
- p11-c-30-s05 · Zoe: Thank you. I’ll keep my phone with me so I can explain it directly.
- p11-c-30-s06 · Receptionist Patel: I have your current number here. I’ll send the callback request now.

- occurrence · n. · 发生；出现的一次事件；义项 sense-staged-ox-42321612058692fd-p11-c-30；来源 ox-42321612058692fd；证据 p11-c-30-s03；词形 occurrence。

审阅者：reviewer-phase11-c；整篇结论：accepted。

Fresh full reread of all six turns, three targets, revised integrated goal and complete evidence: Zoe states first occurrence, daily recurrence and current left-ear ringing. The receptionist only promises to convey the information and request a clinician's call, not to diagnose or guarantee that call. Title, label and repaired goal now agree on forwarding the callback request; the evidence explicitly says no clinician has confirmed a callback. The fictional communication task remains coherent and no completed contact is claimed.

## 决定推迟未成年人俱乐部的户外出游 · p11-c-31

背景：虚构青少年俱乐部的两位成人组织者讨论一次未满十八岁成员的出游，不涉及真实天气预报。

人物：Gemma、Louis。

起草：author-phase11-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p11-c-31-s02 · Louis: And the forecast in our planning sheet gives high odds of heavy rain. Could we switch to an indoor visit?
- p11-c-31-s03 · Gemma: That would be a different activity from the one the families were asked to approve.
- p11-c-31-s04 · Louis: Then I’d opt to postpone rather than rush a new plan through tonight.
- p11-c-31-s05 · Gemma: I agree. Let’s tell everyone tomorrow’s trip is off and ask which dates would work instead.
- p11-c-31-s06 · Louis: Yes. Once we choose a new date, we’ll send the full plan and request consent again.

- opt · v. · 选择；义项 sense-staged-ox-83e3f5e257561713-p11-c-31；来源 ox-83e3f5e257561713；证据 p11-c-31-s04；词形 opt。

审阅者：reviewer-phase11-c；整篇结论：accepted。

The fictional planning exercise explicitly involves under-eighteen members, three missing consents and a forecast in the supplied sheet, not real weather. The decision is complete while notifications, a new date and renewed consent remain future, matching the metadata and evidence.

## 约定限定发明展签的首创说法 · p11-c-33

主题：学习与技能；情景：约定限定发明展签的首创说法（invention-display-origin-scope）。

沟通目标：区分专利记录与发明起源的证据，并约定保留有依据的先驱描述而不宣称最早首创

背景：博物馆教育项目的两位成人学员讨论虚构工程师的档案展签。

人物：Cora、Isaac。

起草：author-phase11-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p11-c-33-s01 · Cora: Did this water-saving valve originate with Ellis? Our label calls her the first person ever to make one.
- p11-c-33-s02 · Isaac: The archive shows a patent in her name from 1924, but it also includes a sketch of a similar valve by another engineer in 1918.
- p11-c-33-s03 · Cora: So the patent doesn’t settle who had the idea first.
- p11-c-33-s04 · Isaac: Right. The local engineering journal calls her a pioneer in bringing the design into everyday use, and gives examples from several buildings.
- p11-c-33-s05 · Cora: Let’s describe that work and its source, but remove the claim that she invented the first valve of its kind.
- p11-c-33-s06 · Isaac: Agreed. I’ll keep the patent date as a separate fact, not proof of priority.

目标用法：

- originate · v. · 起源；始创；义项 sense-staged-ox-bcbc8ed4cd7284bb-p11-c-33；来源 ox-bcbc8ed4cd7284bb；证据 p11-c-33-s01；词形 originate。
- patent · n. · 专利；义项 sense-staged-ox-08c2cc6cd0c57033-p11-c-33；来源 ox-08c2cc6cd0c57033；证据 p11-c-33-s02；词形 patent。
- pioneer · n. · 先驱；开拓者；义项 sense-staged-ox-fceb80f8d637ff83-p11-c-33；来源 ox-fceb80f8d637ff83；证据 p11-c-33-s04；词形 pioneer。

审阅者：reviewer-phase11-c；整篇结论：accepted。

The fictional archive contains both the 1924 patent and a 1918 similar sketch, while a journal supports practical pioneering work. The learners agree to remove absolute priority, retain supported contribution with its source and keep the patent date separately. All metadata clauses are supported; the private contribution explanation is read within that limited contribution claim, not as deleting the separately retained patent fact.

- ox-fceb80f8d637ff83：accepted；s04 a pioneer in bringing the design into everyday use is the selected noun for an early practical innovator, distinguished from absolute first inventor.（p11-c-33-s04）。

- p11-c-35-s01 · Arlo: I’m not sure how to play this pirate. The captain wants to leave him at the next port, but he suddenly starts shouting.
- p11-c-35-s02 · Director Quinn: His plea is for one more voyage with his brother, who is still on the ship. It’s fear of being separated, not a challenge to the captain.
- p11-c-35-s03 · Arlo: So I need to plead with her, not threaten to take over?
- p11-c-35-s04 · Director Quinn: Exactly. Try speaking more quietly and looking toward your brother when you ask to stay.
- p11-c-35-s05 · Arlo: That gives the request a reason. Shall we go back to the captain’s decision?
- p11-c-35-s06 · Director Quinn: Yes. Start there, and let the desperation build without turning it into a threat.

- plea · n. · 恳求；请求；义项 sense-staged-ox-303f02cc7cc5ffc9-p11-c-35；来源 ox-303f02cc7cc5ffc9；证据 p11-c-35-s02；词形 plea。

审阅者：reviewer-phase11-c；整篇结论：accepted。

The actor and director clarify that the pirate fears separation from his brother and should plead rather than threaten the captain. Their final exchange agrees the restart point and quieter approach, not a completed improved performance. Role references, title and all outcome claims remain inside the fictional rehearsal.

## 把剧院筹款承诺与到账款项分开记录 · p11-c-36

主题：社区与公共空间；情景：把剧院筹款承诺与到账款项分开记录（fundraiser-pledge-record）。

沟通目标：核对支持者的捐款承诺尚未到账，并约定只在承诺栏记录而不计入可用资金

背景：虚构社区剧院的两位志愿者核对筹款记录，纯属财务沟通练习。

人物：Val、Andre。

起草：author-phase11-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p11-c-36-s01 · Val: A patron of the theatre has sent a pledge of two hundred pounds. Can I add it to the money available for the new curtains?
- p11-c-36-s02 · Andre: Has the payment arrived, or have we only received the promise?
- p11-c-36-s03 · Val: Only the promise. Her message says she plans to pay next month.
- p11-c-36-s04 · Andre: Then put it in the pledges column, not the amount received. We can’t spend it yet.
- p11-c-36-s05 · Val: Understood. I’ll record the two hundred as promised and leave our available total unchanged.
- p11-c-36-s06 · Andre: Good. We’ll move it across if the payment comes in.

目标用法：

- patron · n. · 资助者；赞助人；义项 sense-staged-ox-3163286a3777e0b6-p11-c-36；来源 ox-3163286a3777e0b6；证据 p11-c-36-s01；词形 patron。
- pledge · n. · 承诺；捐款承诺；义项 sense-staged-ox-6b7ab6c65da0e6c9-p11-c-36；来源 ox-6b7ab6c65da0e6c9；证据 p11-c-36-s01；词形 pledge。

审阅者：reviewer-phase11-c；整篇结论：accepted。

The fictional theatre volunteers explicitly distinguish a two-hundred-pound promise from a received payment. They agree to record it only in pledges and leave available funds unchanged; future transfer of the entry is conditional on payment. Title and evidence describe that recording arrangement without claiming the donation or bookkeeping is complete.

## 约好补发缺少的一节帐篷杆 · p11-c-37

主题：消费与服务；情景：约好补发缺少的一节帐篷杆（camping-pad-pole-replacement）。

沟通目标：核对露营用品中的缺件，并确认店方将补发缺少的一节帐篷杆而无需退回睡垫

背景：顾客在户外用品店展示露营套装内的部件和收据。

人物：Penny、Darius。

起草：author-phase11-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p11-c-37-s01 · Penny: The sleeping pad is fine, but the tent won’t stand properly. I think one pole is missing a section.
- p11-c-37-s02 · Darius: Let’s compare it with the parts diagram. You should have four sections on this side, and I can see only three.
- p11-c-37-s03 · Penny: Do I need to return the whole camping set?
- p11-c-37-s04 · Darius: No. I can order the missing section for you. Keep the pad and the rest of the tent.
- p11-c-37-s05 · Penny: That’s better. Can the section come to this shop so we can check the fit?
- p11-c-37-s06 · Darius: Yes. I’ll order it for collection here and contact you when it arrives.
- p11-c-37-s07 · Penny: Thank you. I’ll keep the other parts together and wait for your call.

- pad · n. · 垫子；睡垫；义项 sense-staged-ox-7176f209d7298f3d-p11-c-37；来源 ox-7176f209d7298f3d；证据 p11-c-37-s01；词形 pad。
- pole · n. · 帐篷杆；支杆；义项 sense-staged-ox-16eaef919ce5b2a0-p11-c-37；来源 ox-16eaef919ce5b2a0；证据 p11-c-37-s01；词形 pole。

审阅者：reviewer-phase11-c；整篇结论：accepted。

The diagram establishes one missing section of a tent pole, while the sleeping pad is intact. The clerk agrees to order that section for shop collection and contact the customer, who accepts. Metadata and private evidence preserve the part-versus-whole distinction and do not claim arrival, fit checking or replacement delivery has occurred.

## 按材料分开战争爆发与战后重建的经历 · p11-c-38

主题：学习与技能；情景：按材料分开战争爆发与战后重建的经历（oral-history-war-period-separation）。

沟通目标：核对口述史中两次迁居属于不同时期，并约定在时间线上分开标注

背景：两位成人学员根据教师提供的虚构口述史制作时间线，不声称讲述真实历史人物。

人物：Nell、Kofi。

起草：author-phase11-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p11-c-38-s01 · Nell: I put the family’s move beside the outbreak of war, but the next paragraph describes postwar rebuilding. Have I mixed two events together?
- p11-c-38-s02 · Kofi: Yes. The interview says they left the village when war began, then moved back to the city after it ended.
- p11-c-38-s03 · Nell: So the first move was away from the fighting, and the second was part of starting again afterward.
- p11-c-38-s04 · Kofi: That’s how the speaker describes it. We should keep those two reasons and periods separate.
- p11-c-38-s05 · Nell: I’ll put them on different lines and use the dates given in the interview.
- p11-c-38-s06 · Kofi: Good. Then readers won’t mistake the return for the original departure.

目标用法：

- outbreak · n. · 爆发；义项 sense-staged-ox-8b25f8f46c538be2-p11-c-38；来源 ox-8b25f8f46c538be2；证据 p11-c-38-s01；词形 outbreak。
- postwar · adj. · 战后的（无连字符拼写）；义项 sense-staged-ox-d6e00d6dd31f2445-p11-c-38；来源 ox-d6e00d6dd31f2445；证据 p11-c-38-s01；词形 postwar。

审阅者：reviewer-phase11-c；整篇结论：accepted。

The fictional oral-history exercise separates departure when war began from return to the city after it ended. The source is said to supply distinct reasons and dates; Nell agrees to place the two events separately and Kofi accepts. The title is the editing task, while the goal/evidence correctly mark the timeline work as agreed rather than finished.
